# Main shortlist: team research and chance of positive return

Generated (UTC): 2026-11-08T12:20:11.356382+00:00. Picks: 85.

One pick per fixture and market group. Highest probability of a positive net return first; expected return then offered odds break ties. Pushes are not wins. No minimum edge or forced quota.

Recent ten-match form is weighted newest first, with home/away splits and supplied xG. Form is shrunk toward the fitted team goal rates with a ten-match prior. Qualitative news is evidence for review, without unvalidated probability adjustments. Missing research stays visible and does not exclude a match. Model probabilities are unvalidated estimates.

## Master shortlist (15 profit-first nominations when available)

One market per match; DC 1X/X2 nominations use actual Pinnacle AH +0.5 quotes, DC 12 is excluded. Over 1.5 is excluded. Local training and researched form/xG inform source forecasts/nominations; master probabilities/ranking remain Pinnacle-referenced. Weather, team news, rest/travel, H2H, tactics and motivation are review context, with no unvalidated numerical adjustment. Fresh market references and valid execution evidence are required. There is no minimum EV or probability gate. Ranked by Pinnacle-referenced profit probability, market net EV, no-loss probability and odds. Refunds are not wins. Negative-EV nominations remain visible and flagged; they are not proven value bets. This selection rule is unvalidated; suggested stakes are zero. Model comparisons do not affect ranking or reward missing data. Quotes up to 15 minutes old are allowed with ages and re-check warnings; all prices must be re-checked before betting. This run: 15 nominations; target shortfall 0; 15 negative market EV. Review master_shortlist_audit.csv for exclusions.
